{
  "file_path": "/Users/arijit/Coding/Python/DataQ-harness/airflow/dags/flow_a_snowflake_load.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Flow A — load to warehouse (Snowflake). §3a.\n\nScheduled load (the schedule-trigger modality): reads each Snowflake-bound feed from\nthe ADLS landing zone and overwrites the target table, then the DAG-level success\ncallback POSTs to DataQ → DataQ records the pipeline_run and fires any suite bound to\nthis DAG (`flow_a_snowflake_load`). Offset to run AFTER the file-creation Jobs (§2a).\n\nRequired env (set on the Airflow Container App, key-based):\n  AZURE_STORAGE_CONNECTION_STRING        ADLS read\n  SNOWFLAKE_ACCOUNT/USER/PASSWORD/WAREHOUSE/DATABASE[/SCHEMA/ROLE]\n  DATAQ_WEBHOOK_URL / DATAQ_WEBHOOK_SECRET / DATAQ_AIRFLOW_BASE_URL   trigger callback\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pendulum\nfrom airflow import DAG\nfrom airflow.operators.python import PythonOperator\nfrom dataq_airflow_callback import on_dataq_failure, on_dataq_success\n\nfrom _harness import load_to_snowflake, read_feed\n\n# Snowflake-bound feeds (route map §2): subdir under the landing zone -> table name.\n# All land as CSV (COPY-INTO-friendly in §2).\nSNOWFLAKE_FEEDS = [\n    \"orders_header\",\n    \"order_lines\",\n    \"payments\",\n    \"refunds\",\n    \"returns\",\n    \"customers\",\n    \"products\",\n]\n\n\ndef _load(feed: str, **_: object) -> None:\n    df = read_feed(f\"snowflake/{feed}\", \"csv\")\n    if not df.empty:\n        load_to_snowflake(df, feed)\n\n\nwith DAG(\n    dag_id=\"flow_a_snowflake_load\",\n    description=\"DataQ harness Flow A — load retail feeds from ADLS into Snowflake\",\n    schedule=\"30 1 * * *\",  # daily 01:30 UTC — after the §2a file-creation Jobs\n    start_date=pendulum.datetime(2026, 6, 1, tz=\"UTC\"),\n    catchup=False,\n    on_success_callback=on_dataq_success,\n    on_failure_callback=on_dataq_failure,\n    tags=[\"dataq-harness\", \"flow-a\", \"snowflake\"],\n):\n    for _feed in SNOWFLAKE_FEEDS:\n        PythonOperator(\n            task_id=f\"load_{_feed}\",\n            python_callable=_load,\n            op_kwargs={\"feed\": _feed},\n        )\n"
}

  "content": "# DataQ harness — Airflow (§3 load + trigger)\n\nThe **Clock-2** orchestrator (load + trigger), hosted on Container Apps (see\n`terraform/airflow.tf`). 151	
152	## 3. Reference flows (each exercises a distinct DataQ run path)
153	
154	### 3a. Flow A — load to warehouse  →  `SnowflakeCheckRunner` / `UnityCatalogCheckRunner`
155	
156	> **Datasets:** **Snowflake** ← Orders (header+line) · Payments/Finance · Returns/Cancellations · Customer master · Product/SKU master (transactional + financial core; custom-SQL + line-grain + `sum(line_total)=order_total` + `order_total↔payment↔refund` reconciliation). **Unity Catalog (plain load)** ← Location · Promotions/pricing/coupons · Channel/Marketplace (governed reference data — proves the UC runner *without* the medallion machinery).
157	
158	- [ ] File lands in ADLS landing zone (from §2 generator)
159	- [ ] **ADF pipeline** (or Airflow DAG) picks the file up and loads it into Snowflake / UC
160	- [ ] On success, the orchestrator hits DataQ's trigger path (ADF webhook / Airflow callback → trigger binding)
161	- [ ] DataQ suite runs against the loaded **warehouse table**; result row persists
162	- [ ] Verify in `/results`: run succeeded, severity derived, metric_value populated
163	- [ ] **Snowflake suite** exercises a **custom-SQL** check (ADR 0019) — e.g. the `sum(line_total)=order_total` / `order_total↔payment↔refund` aggregate — not just column expectations
164	- [ ] **UC plain-load suite** runs on a Location / Promotions table (no Silver/Gold transform — that's Flow B)
165	
166	### 3b. Flow B — medallion  →  UC tables
167	
168	> **Datasets:** **Feedback/survey/chatbot** (bronze raw JSON/text → silver cleaned + **PII-handled** [#226] → gold sentiment/NPS feature table — the lakehouse/ML "feature store" story) and **Sales → revenue marts** (bronze raw sales events → silver cleaned daily sales by store·channel → gold revenue/units rollups).
169	
170	- [ ] File lands in **UC RAW (bronze)** layer
171	- [ ] **Databricks notebook** (external, not git-tracked) runs: bronze → silver → gold transforms
172	- [ ] DataQ suites run against the UC Silver/Gold tables
